<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt; text-transform: uppercase"><b>NATIONAL
ENERGY SERVICES REUNITED CORP. AND SUBSIDIARIES</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="m_007"></span>CONSOLIDATED

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="m_008"></span>NOTES

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000466" name="us-gaap:BasisOfAccounting"><p id="xdx_807_eus-gaap--BasisOfAccounting_zLI9I6P65TRi" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="m_009"></span>1.
<span id="xdx_82A_zevo4RydkWZj">BASIS OF PRESENTATION</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
accompanying consolidated financial statements of National Energy Services Reunited Corp. (the &#8220;Company,&#8221;
&#8220;NESR,&#8221; &#8220;we,&#8221; &#8220;our,&#8221; &#8220;us,&#8221; or similar terms) have been prepared in accordance with
accounting principles generally accepted in the United States of America (&#8220;U.S. GAAP&#8221;) for interim financial
information. Accordingly, they do not include all of the information and footnotes required by U.S. GAAP for complete financial
statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
the opinion of NESR management, all adjustments (consisting of normal recurring adjustments) considered necessary for a fair presentation
have been included in the accompanying financial statements. All intercompany transactions and balances have been eliminated
in consolidation. All amounts are shown in thousands ($000s) of U.S. dollars, except as noted.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Operating
results for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, are not necessarily indicative of the results that may be expected for the full
year ending December 31, 2026. The balance sheet as of December 31, 2025 has been derived from NESR&#8217;s audited financial statements
for the year then ended.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">For
further information, refer to the Consolidated Financial Statements and related notes included in NESR&#8217;s Annual Report on Form
10-K for the year ended December 31, 2025, filed with the Securities and Exchange Commission on March 6, 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000496" name="NESR:ProductionManagementAssetsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_846_ecustom--ProductionManagementAssetsPolicyTextBlock_zry54JpmASA2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span id="xdx_860_zZH0hTlFN6ok">Production
Management Assets</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company&#8217;s Integrated Production Management (&#8220;IPM&#8221;) projects are focused on developing and managing production on behalf
of the Company&#8217;s customers under long-term agreements. Under these arrangements, the Company contributes its own services and products
and, in certain cases, cash, toward the customer&#8217;s field development activities and operations. Although in certain arrangements
the Company is paid for a portion of the services or products it provides, generally the Company will not be paid at the time of providing
its services or upon delivery of its products. Instead, the Company is compensated based on cash flow generated by cash from the customer&#8217;s
wells. Revenues from IPM arrangements, which is recognized as the related production is achieved, represented  <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--PropertyManagementFeePercentFee_pid_dp_uPure_c20260101__20260331_zzjggCMqV7jb" title="Property management fee"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PropertyManagementFeePercentFee" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000498" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">0.0</ix:nonFraction></span>% (zero) and <span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--PropertyManagementFeePercentFee_pid_dp_uPure_c20250101__20250331_z9ecX8ut6MUf" title="Property management fee"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PropertyManagementFeePercentFee" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000500" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">0.8</ix:nonFraction></span>%
of the Company&#8217;s Revenues for the three-month periods ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"></p>

<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 8; Value: 2 -->
    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->8<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>
    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company capitalizes its cash investments in a project as well as the direct costs associated with providing services or products for
which the Company will be compensated when the related production is achieved. These capitalized investments are amortized to the Consolidated Statements of Operations as the related production is achieved based on the units of production method, whereby
each unit produced is assigned a pro-rata portion of the unamortized costs based on estimated total production, resulting in a matching
of revenue with the applicable costs. Amortization expense relating to these capitalized investments was $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--CapitalizedContractCostAmortization_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331_zq5Cu0ulhOzc" title="Capitalized investments"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CapitalizedContractCostAmortization" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000502" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.0</ix:nonFraction></span> (zero) million and $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--CapitalizedContractCostAmortization_pn5n6_c20250101__20250331_zNtE3LLQIFtg" title="Capitalized investments"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CapitalizedContractCostAmortization" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000504" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.9</ix:nonFraction></span> million
for the three-month periods ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
unamortized portion of the Company&#8217;s investments in IPM projects was $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentUnamortizedPremium_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zGvFnx80XCFk" title="Unamortized investments"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentUnamortizedPremium" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000506" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.0</ix:nonFraction></span> (zero) million and $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentUnamortizedPremium_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_zrC3QZMFPI7l" title="Unamortized investments"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentUnamortizedPremium" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000508" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.0</ix:nonFraction></span> (zero) million at March 31, 2026,
and December 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">At
March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, the Company assessed whether the unamortized costs associated with these investments exceed the present
value of future cash flows from the projects, and recorded an impairment charge to Cost of Services of $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--AssetImpairmentCharges_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331_zI7gLs8hAiAb" title="Impairment charges"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetImpairmentCharges" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000510" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.0</ix:nonFraction></span> (zero) million and $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--AssetImpairmentCharges_pn5n6_c20250101__20250331_zjDOq5ZEvII1" title="Impairment charges"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetImpairmentCharges" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000512" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.1</ix:nonFraction></span> million
for the three-month periods ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000514" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84D_eus-gaap--NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zeta1oLSssq7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Recently
issued accounting standards not yet adopted</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
November 4, 2024, the FASB issued ASU No. 2024-03, Income Statement&#8212;Reporting Comprehensive Income&#8212;Expense Disaggregation
Disclosures (Subtopic 220-40): <i>Disaggregation of Income Statement Expenses </i>(the &#8220;ASU&#8221;), which requires disaggregated
disclosure of income statement expenses for public business entities (&#8220;PBEs&#8221;). The ASU does not change the expense captions
an entity presents on the face of the income statement; rather, it requires disaggregation of certain expense captions into specified
categories in disclosures within the footnotes to the financial statements. ASU 2024-03 is effective for all PBEs for fiscal years beginning
after December 15, 2026, and interim periods within fiscal years beginning after December 15, 2027. Early adoption is permitted. The
Company is currently reviewing the impact of the adoption on the consolidated financial statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">All
new accounting pronouncements that have been issued but not yet effective are currently being evaluated and, at this time, are not expected
to have a material impact on our financial position or results of operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The

period ended March 31, 2025, respectively, within Cost of Services in the Consolidated Statements of Operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>2021
Secured Facilities Agreement</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
November 4, 2021, the Company entered into an $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_pn6n6_c20211104__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_zpv62nTI7Bsi"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2021-11-04_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000637" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">860</ix:nonFraction></span></span> <span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">million
Secured Facilities Agreement (the &#8220;2021 Secured Facilities Agreement&#8221;). At inception, the 2021 Secured Facilities
Agreement consisted of a $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentPeriodicPayment_pn6n6_c20211104__20211104__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_zSkHAm9dXzS2"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentPeriodicPayment" contextRef="From2021-11-042021-11-04_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000638" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">430</ix:nonFraction></span></span> <span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">million
term loan due by November 4, 2027 (the &#8220;Term Loan&#8221; or &#8220;Secured Term Loan&#8221;), a $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityPeriodicPayment_pn5n6_c20211104__20211104__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_zleiH2LbuQQ8"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityPeriodicPayment" contextRef="From2021-11-042021-11-04_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000639" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">80.0</ix:nonFraction></span></span> <span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">million
revolving credit facility due by November 4, 2025 (&#8220;RCF&#8221; or &#8220;Secured Revolving Credit Facility&#8221;), and a
$<span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--WorkingCapitalFacility_iI_pn6n6_c20211104__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--LendersMember_zhA1bbpTpGIl"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:WorkingCapitalFacility" contextRef="AsOf2021-11-04_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_custom_LendersMember" id="Fact000640" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">350</ix:nonFraction></span></span> <span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">million
working capital facility that renews annually or bi-annually by mutual agreement between the lenders and the Company. The Secured
Revolving Credit Facility was extended from November 4, 2025, to February 4, 2026, by mutual agreement of the Company and its
lenders. Following February 4, 2026, the Company did not extend its Secured Revolving Credit Facility, as operating cash flow,
together with available cash and cash equivalents, is sufficient to support working capital, capital expenditures, and debt
repayment obligations. The Company is currently working with its lenders to refinance and extend the 2021 Secured Facilities
Agreement.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

(zero) million and $<span id="xdx_907_ecustom--WithdrawnTermLoan_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_ztWlLgxDZJM8" title="Withdrawn term loan"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:WithdrawnTermLoan" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000660" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.0</ix:nonFraction></span>

Additionally, as of March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, the Company had $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_z3INkYVMUMCk" title="Borrowing capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000662" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.0</ix:nonFraction></span>
(zero) million and $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_zYNgrh9UDTf5" title="Borrowing capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000664" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">59.2</ix:nonFraction></span>
million, respectively, available to be drawn under the RCF.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white">The
2021 Secured Facilities Agreement also includes a working capital facility of $<span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--WorkingCapitalFacility_iI_pn6n6_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_zuAB02DiHfsc" title="Working capital facility"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:WorkingCapitalFacility" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000666" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">259</ix:nonFraction></span>
million and $<span id="xdx_901_ecustom--WorkingCapitalFacility_iI_pn6n6_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_zrv3eHtjxFb2" title="Working capital facility"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:WorkingCapitalFacility" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000668" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">325</ix:nonFraction></span>
million as of March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, respectively, for issuance of letters of guarantee, letters of credit and
refinancing letters of credit into debt over a period of no more than two years, which carries an interest rate equal to SOFR for
U.S. dollar-denominated borrowings, or SAIBOR for Saudi Arabia Riyal borrowings, for the applicable interest period, plus a margin
of <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_pid_dp_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MinimumMember_z79IqsJdHg47" title="Interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_srt_MinimumMember" id="Fact000670" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">1.25</ix:nonFraction></span>%
to <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_pid_dp_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MaximumMember_zQbH1YdzmJFa" title="Interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_srt_MaximumMember" id="Fact000672" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">1.5</ix:nonFraction></span>%
per annum. The working capital facility decreased by $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityAnnualPrincipalPayment_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_zC4xi5bDuTZd" title="Decreasing working capital capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityAnnualPrincipalPayment" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000674" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">66.0</ix:nonFraction></span>
million during the quarter ended March 31, 2026, due to the scheduled expiration of one tranche of availability within the lender
syndicate. As of March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, this resulted in interest rates of <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateAtPeriodEnd_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331_zduZQckf9U78" title="Line of credit interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateAtPeriodEnd" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000676" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">6.52</ix:nonFraction></span>%
and <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateAtPeriodEnd_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20251231_zD15nRf4Vwyd" title="Line of credit interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateAtPeriodEnd" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000678" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">7.13</ix:nonFraction></span>%,
respectively, for U.S. dollar-denominated borrowings, and interest rates of <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateDuringPeriod_pid_dp_uPure_c20260101__20260331__srt--StatementGeographicalAxis__country--SA_z6orEKW0F0vc" title="Line of credit facility interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateDuringPeriod" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_country_SA" id="Fact000680" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">7.44</ix:nonFraction></span>%
and <span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateDuringPeriod_pid_dp_uPure_c20250101__20251231__srt--StatementGeographicalAxis__country--SA_zz41YKbvcF01" title="Line of credit facility interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityInterestRateDuringPeriod" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_country_SA" id="Fact000682" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">8.18</ix:nonFraction></span>%,
respectively, for Saudi Arabian Riyal borrowings. The working capital facility requires the payment of a commitment fee each
quarter. The commitment fee is computed at the rate of <span id="xdx_908_ecustom--CommitmentFeeRate_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_zOjXwQFSJf3" title="Commitment fee rate"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:CommitmentFeeRate" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000684" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">0.3125</ix:nonFraction></span>%
(<span id="xdx_906_ecustom--MarginCommitmentFeeRate_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__us-gaap--RevolvingCreditFacilityMember_zcKLd1WmnTR8" title="Margin commitment fee rate"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:MarginCommitmentFeeRate" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RevolvingCreditFacilityMember" id="Fact000686" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">25</ix:nonFraction></span>%
of the margin) on the facility lender&#8217;s available commitment for the relevant quarter. As of March 31, 2026, and December 31,
2025, the Company had utilized $<span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--UtilizedWorkingCapitalFacility_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_zuHZH7LhxOt" title="Utilized working capital"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:UtilizedWorkingCapitalFacility" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000688" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">241.7</ix:nonFraction></span>
million and $<span id="xdx_90E_ecustom--UtilizedWorkingCapitalFacility_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_z1aQADhZqR28" title="Utilized working capital"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:UtilizedWorkingCapitalFacility" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000690" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">243.8</ix:nonFraction></span>
million, respectively, under this working capital facility and the balance of $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_zqxUxHyg25Me" title="Working capital borrowing capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000692" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">17.3</ix:nonFraction></span>
million and $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_z5IuM2QRkAzb" title="Working capital borrowing capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000694" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">81.2</ix:nonFraction></span>
million, respectively, was available to the Company.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentCovenantDescription_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember_z2xkrFutRIaf" title="Debt covenants description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_SecuredFacilitiesAgreementMember" id="Fact000696" name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentCovenantDescription">The
2021 Secured Facilities Agreement includes covenants that specify maximum leverage (Net Debt / EBITDA) up to 3.50, minimum debt service
coverage ratio (Cash Flow / Debt Service) of at least 1.25, and interest coverage (EBITDA / Interest) of at least 4.00.</ix:nonNumeric></span> As of March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, the Company was in compliance with all financial and non-financial covenants under the 2021 Secured Facilities Agreement.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000725" name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_803_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxDisclosureTextBlock_z1oEoTZl2a15" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="m_015"></span>7.
<span id="xdx_82B_zNSkOykEoO">INCOME TAXES</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">NESR is a holding company
incorporated in the British Virgin Islands (&#8220;BVI&#8221;), which imposes a 0% statutory corporate income tax rate on income generated outside the jurisdiction. NESR&#8217;s
subsidiaries operate across multiple tax jurisdictions in the MENA region, where statutory tax rates generally range from <span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate_pid_dp_uPure_c20260101__20260331__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MinimumMember_ztUaTvZdwk11" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_srt_MinimumMember" id="Fact000727" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">0</ix:nonFraction></span>% to <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate_pid_dp_uPure_c20260101__20260331__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MaximumMember_zT8pMx3Qx3j4" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_srt_MaximumMember" id="Fact000729" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">35</ix:nonFraction></span>%. However,
the adoption of Domestic Minimum Top-Up Taxes (DMTT) under the OECD Pillar Two framework has, in many cases, resulted in effective tax
rates exceeding local statutory minimums.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white">The
Company recorded income tax expense of $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331_zI74LzLWnAGe" title="Income tax expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000731" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">7.1</ix:nonFraction></span> million with an effective tax rate of <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate_pid_dp_uPure_c20260101__20260331_zbhEnwfAL6Ig" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000733" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">23.0</ix:nonFraction></span>%, and $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit_pn5n6_c20250101__20250331_ztQSxrsDAcw" title="Income tax expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000735" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">3.3</ix:nonFraction></span> million with an effective tax rate of
<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate_pid_dp_uPure_c20250101__20250331_zcb0DPIQl7N6" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000737" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">24.2</ix:nonFraction></span>%, for the three-month periods ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively, in the Consolidated Statements of Operations.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white">The
period-on-period decrease in the effective tax rate is primarily driven by changes in geographic earnings mix, as well as higher pre-tax
income relative to adjustments related to the Company&#8217;s uncertain tax positions and unrecognized tax benefits.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000739" name="us-gaap:CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80C_eus-gaap--CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock_z0ZwzJ8PMgl" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="m_016"></span>8.
<span id="xdx_825_zZG3qYMYHtT4">COMMITMENTS AND CONTINGENCIES</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Capital
expenditure commitments</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">The Company was committed
to incur capital expenditures of $<span id="xdx_904_ecustom--CapitalExpendituresIncurred_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zOlBqsTq1Zo9" title="Capital expenditure"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:CapitalExpendituresIncurred" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000741" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">75.8</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--CapitalExpendituresIncurred_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_zk5cZ0sih547" title="Capital expenditure"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:CapitalExpendituresIncurred" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000743" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">45.6</ix:nonFraction></span> million at March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, respectively. Substantially
all of the commitments outstanding as of March 31, 2026, are expected to be settled during 2026 and 2027.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Other
commitments</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">The Company purchases certain
property, plant, and equipment using seller-provided installment financing with payment terms extending to 24 months. As of March 31,
2026, and December 31, 2025, the Company recorded $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--OtherLiabilities_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__custom--BalanceSheetLocationsAxis__us-gaap--AccountsPayableMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__us-gaap--RelatedPartyMember_zjKkQi2KUjbh"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherLiabilities" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_AccountsPayableMember_us-gaap_RelatedPartyMember" id="Fact000744" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">5.2</ix:nonFraction></span>
million and $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--OtherLiabilities_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__custom--BalanceSheetLocationsAxis__us-gaap--AccountsPayableMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__us-gaap--RelatedPartyMember_zqvPouHONdHj"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherLiabilities" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_AccountsPayableMember_us-gaap_RelatedPartyMember" id="Fact000745" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">2.1</ix:nonFraction></span>
million, respectively, in Accounts payable for amounts due using seller-provided installment financing.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">The Company had outstanding
letters of credit amounting to $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--LettersOfCreditOutstandingAmount_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zJvaIvanL9K8" title="Outstanding letters of credit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LettersOfCreditOutstandingAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000747" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">7.2</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--LettersOfCreditOutstandingAmount_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_z5V2wuHMJyb3" title="Outstanding letters of credit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LettersOfCreditOutstandingAmount" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000749" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">6.6</ix:nonFraction></span> million as of March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, respectively.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 27.5pt">In the normal course of business
with customers, vendors and others, the Company has entered into off-balance sheet arrangements, such as surety bonds for performance,
and other bank issued guarantees which totaled $<span id="xdx_901_ecustom--SuretyBondsAndOtherBankIssuedGuarantees_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zYVobf4cRyU1" title="Surety bonds and other bank issued guarantees"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:SuretyBondsAndOtherBankIssuedGuarantees" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000751" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">193.3</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_90F_ecustom--SuretyBondsAndOtherBankIssuedGuarantees_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_z1LRApUwagpd" title="Surety bonds and other bank issued guarantees"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:SuretyBondsAndOtherBankIssuedGuarantees" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000753" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">187.1</ix:nonFraction></span> million as of March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, respectively.
The Company has also entered into cash margin guarantees totaling $<span id="xdx_906_ecustom--CashMarginGuarantees_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zjRaKPFTFnz9" title="Cash margin guarantees"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:CashMarginGuarantees" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000755" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">16.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_90F_ecustom--CashMarginGuarantees_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_z1tgiAD4Yjvj" title="Cash margin guarantees"><ix:nonFraction name="NESR:CashMarginGuarantees" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000757" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">14.9</ix:nonFraction></span> million at March 31, 2026, and December 31,
2025, respectively. A liability is accrued when a loss is both probable and can be reasonably estimated. Cash margin guarantees totaling
$<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--RestrictedCash_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zvM5dV7WbNX4" title="Restricted cash deposits"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RestrictedCash" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000759" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">8.7</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--RestrictedCash_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_z6r6xXlTo2y2" title="Restricted cash deposits"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RestrictedCash" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000761" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">7.9</ix:nonFraction></span> million have been secured by restricted cash deposits at March 31, 2026, and December 31, 2025, respectively. None
of the off-balance sheet arrangements either has, or is likely to have, a material effect on the Company&#8217;s consolidated financial
statements.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 27.5pt"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Legal
proceedings</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
the first quarter of 2024, the Company lost control (i.e., ceased to be the primary beneficiary) of a subsidiary in Qatar following
a judgment from a lawsuit concerning the ownership and historical profits of this entity. Neither the deconsolidation event nor any
potential losses associated with these historical profits were deemed material to the Company&#8217;s financial position or results
of operations. Subsequent to the judgment, the plaintiff initiated similar actions against other unrelated Company entities in both
Qatar and the United Arab Emirates. Based on the advice of legal counsel in each jurisdiction, the Company believes it has strong
grounds to defend its positions in both jurisdictions; however, the ultimate outcome of these proceedings cannot be determined at
this time. The Company currently estimates that a loss in the range of $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--GainLossOnSaleOfBusiness_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MinimumMember_zBi1vtRBnO4d" title="Company loss"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfBusiness" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_srt_MinimumMember" id="Fact000763" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.2</ix:nonFraction></span>
million to $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--GainLossOnSaleOfBusiness_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MaximumMember_zfMLlg43Hrda" title="Company loss"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfBusiness" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_srt_MaximumMember" id="Fact000765" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">11.8</ix:nonFraction></span>
million (<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--GainLossOnSaleOfBusiness_pn5n6_uQAR_c20260101__20260331__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MaximumMember_z5nmv5OMD21c" title="Company loss"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfBusiness" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_srt_MaximumMember" id="Fact000767" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="QAR">43.1</ix:nonFraction></span>
million Qatari Riyals), for which it is jointly and severally liable with a co-defendant, could be incurred in connection with these
matters.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Additionally,
the Company is involved in certain legal proceedings which arise in the ordinary course of business and the outcomes of which are currently
subject to uncertainties and therefore the probability of a loss, if any, being sustained and an estimate of the amount of any loss are
difficult to ascertain. Consequently, it is not possible to make a reasonable estimate of the expected financial effect, if any, that
will result from ultimate resolution of these disputes. The Company is contesting these claims/disputes and the Company&#8217;s management
currently believes that it is not required to recognize a provision because they are not probable or reasonably estimable and any impacts
are not expected to have a material impact on the Company&#8217;s business, financial condition, results of operations, or liquidity.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000769" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerShareTextBlock"><p id="xdx_802_eus-gaap--EarningsPerShareTextBlock_zjmT7tvtRiBf" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="m_017"></span>9.
<span id="xdx_826_zOOZ7KAUw8ci">EARNINGS PER SHARE</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

    <td style="border-bottom: Black 2.5pt double; font-weight: bold; text-align: left">$</td><td id="xdx_989_eus-gaap--IncomeLossFromContinuingOperationsPerDilutedShare_pid_c20250101__20250331_z8xEHFi3RYCc" title="Weighted-average ordinary shares outstanding, Diluted" style="border-bottom: Black 2.5pt double; font-weight: bold; text-align: right"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeLossFromContinuingOperationsPerDilutedShare" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000880" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.11</ix:nonFraction></td><td style="padding-bottom: 2.5pt; font-weight: bold; text-align: left">&#160;</td></tr>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8AD_zbQHupVKoPw2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">Prior to the announcement
of the exchange offer on May 30, 2025, potentially dilutive warrants had no impact on the determination of dilutive earnings per share
as these potential ordinary shares were antidilutive for the three-month period ended March 31, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.5in">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">In addition to the warrants,
the Company excluded <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_pid_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_zcndhjPMGLPi" title="Number of shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact000882" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">896,880</ix:nonFraction></span> and <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_pid_c20250101__20250331__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_z045WZmHsImi" title="Number of shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact000884" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">2,019,453</ix:nonFraction></span> weighted-average restricted stock units from dilutive ordinary shares for the three-month periods
ended March 31, 2026, and 2025, respectively, as they were assumed to be repurchased due to the impact of unrecognized share-based compensation
cost.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000886" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_804_eus-gaap--SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock_zZIQOagey6zj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="m_018"></span>10.
<span id="xdx_829_zwU0f4vD6VY8">REPORTABLE SEGMENTS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company determines its operating segments using the management approach, under which its Chief Executive Officer, as the
Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker, regularly reviews financial information to allocate resources and assess performance. The Company has
identified <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--NumberOfReportableSegments_dc_uSegments_c20260101__20260331_zZzP07ZBmwkj" title="Number of reportable segments"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NumberOfReportableSegments" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000888" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="INF" unitRef="Segments">two</ix:nonFraction></span>
reportable segments, Production Services and Drilling and Evaluation Services, which provide a range of oilfield services to oil and
natural gas customers across the well lifecycle. Segment performance is evaluated primarily based on revenue and segment operating
income (loss), excluding corporate-level expenses such as overhead, share-based compensation, and transaction-related costs. The
Company&#8217;s operations are primarily located in the MENA region.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.5in"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Unallocated
expenses for the three-month periods ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively, mainly include corporate selling, general,
and administrative expenses (inclusive of amortization), offset in small part by a portion of these costs that are allocated to the reportable
segments. As described elsewhere, corporate selling, general, and administrative expenses are primarily comprised of payroll and compensation
costs for headquarters&#8217; employees, professional and legal expenses relating to audit firms, consulting firms and legal counsel,
and depreciation charges on headquarters&#8217; offices and leasehold improvements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="sq_001"></span>ITEM
2. MANAGEMENT&#8217;S DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS OF FINANCIAL CONDITION AND RESULTS OF OPERATIONS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>The
following discussion and analysis should be read in conjunction with the consolidated financial statements
and related notes included in this Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q (&#8220;Quarterly Report&#8221;). In addition, such analysis should
be read in conjunction with the audited consolidated financial statements, the related notes, and the other information included in the
Company&#8217;s Annual Report on Form 10-K for the year ended December 31, 2025 (&#8220;2025 Annual Report&#8221;). The following discussion and
analysis contain forward-looking statements that reflect our future plans, estimates, beliefs and expected performance. Please read &#8220;Cautionary
Note Regarding Forward-Looking Statements&#8221; below.</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">National
Energy Services Reunited Corp. (the &#8220;Company,&#8221; &#8220;NESR,&#8221; &#8220;we,&#8221; &#8220;our,&#8221; &#8220;us&#8221;
or similar terms) is one of the largest oilfield services providers in the Middle East and North Africa (&#8220;MENA&#8221;) region.
The Company&#8217;s business consists primarily of upstream and midstream oilfield services with oil and natural gas companies as
customers. NESR&#8217;s revenues are primarily derived by providing production services (&#8220;Production Services&#8221;) such as
hydraulic fracturing, coiled tubing, stimulation and pumping, cementing, nitrogen services, filtration services, pipelines and
industrial services, production assurance, artificial lift services, completions and integrated production management. NESR also
provides drilling and evaluation services (&#8220;Drilling and Evaluation Services&#8221;) such as rigs and integrated services,
fishing and downhole tools, thru-tubing intervention, tubular running services, directional drilling, drilling and completion
fluids, pressure control, well testing services, wireline logging services, and slickline services. NESR has significant operations
throughout the MENA region including Saudi Arabia, Oman, Kuwait, United Arab Emirates (&#8220;UAE&#8221;), Iraq, Algeria, Egypt and
Libya.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="sq_002"></span>EXECUTIVE
OVERVIEW</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Drivers
of Our Financial Condition and Results of Operations</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As of the end of the three-month period covered by this Quarterly Report,
the ongoing conflict between the United States, Israel and Iran has contributed to heightened volatility in global crude oil markets and
increased upstream activity across the MENA region, as reflected in the increase in Brent crude prices and the regional rig count discussed
below. While these conditions have favorably impacted customer demand for our services during the period, the duration, scope and ultimate
trajectory of the conflict remain uncertain, and a de-escalation, a further escalation, or related sanctions, supply disruptions or geopolitical
responses could materially affect commodity prices, customer capital spending and our results of operations and financial condition in
future periods. For a full discussion of the drivers of our financial condition and results
of operations, see the section entitled &#8220;<i>Drivers of Our Financial Condition and Results of Operations</i>&#8221; in Part II,
Item 7 of our 2025 Annual Report.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Key
Performance Indicators</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As of the end of the three-month period covered by this Quarterly Report,
there have been no material changes regarding our key performance indicators except as provided in the following table which shows rig
count (Source: Baker Hughes Published Rig Count Data) and oil prices (Source: U.S. Energy Information Administration - Brent &#8211; Europe)
as of the dates indicated. For a full discussion of our key performance indicators, see the section entitled &#8220;<i>Key Performance
Indicators</i>&#8221; in Part II, Item 7 of our 2025 Annual Report.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->18<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="sq_003"></span>RESULTS
OF OPERATIONS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">We
operate our business through two operating segments and report our results of operations through two reporting segments, Production Services
and Drilling and Evaluation Services, which aggregate services performed during distinct stages of a typical life cycle of an oil well.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Production
Services</i>. Our Production Services segment includes the results of operations from services that are generally offered and performed
during the production stage of a well&#8217;s lifecycle. These services mainly include hydraulic fracturing, coiled tubing, stimulation
and pumping, cementing, nitrogen services, filtration services, pipelines and industrial services, production assurance, artificial lift
services, completions and integrated production management. Our Production Services accounted for 60% and 62% of our revenues for the
three-month periods ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Drilling
and Evaluation Services</i>. Our Drilling and Evaluation Services segment includes the results of operations from services that are generally
offered and performed during pre-production stages of a well&#8217;s lifecycle and related mainly to the operation of oil rigs. The services
mainly include rigs and integrated services, fishing and downhole tools, thru-tubing intervention, tubular running services, directional
drilling, drilling and completion fluids, pressure control, well testing services, wireline logging services and slickline services.
Our Drilling and Evaluation Services accounted for 40% and 38% of our revenues for the three-month periods ended March 31, 2026, and
March 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">For a full discussion of our reportable segments, see the section entitled
&#8220;<i>Business</i>&#8221; in Part I, Item 1 of our 2025 Annual Report.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Key
Components of Revenues and Expenses</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As of the end of the three-month period covered by this Quarterly Report,
there have been no material changes to our key components of revenues and expenses. See the section entitled &#8220;<i>Key Components
of Revenues and Expenses</i>&#8221; in Part II, Item 7 of our 2025 Annual Report for more information.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></span></p>

compared to 2025</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 5; Value: 15 -->
    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->19<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>
    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">Production Services revenue
was $241.0 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $188.1 million for the three-month period ended March
31, 2025. The change in revenue was primarily due to increased hydraulic fracturing stages in Saudi Arabia.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in">Drilling and Evaluation Services
revenue was $163.5 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $115.0 million for the three-month period ended
March 31, 2025. The change in revenue
was primarily due to increased well testing activity in Saudi Arabia.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><b><i>Cost of services. </i></b>Cost
of services was $352.8 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $265.6 million for the three-month period
ended March 31, 2025. Cost of services as a percentage of total revenue was 87.2% and 87.6% for the three-month periods ended March 31,
2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively. The change in cost of services as a percentage of total revenue is mainly due to increased activity
levels in the period ended March 31, 2026, as compared to the prior year period, reflecting improved cost absorption as revenue scaled. Cost of services included depreciation expense of $29.1
million, and $29.5 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025, respectively.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><b><i>Gross profit</i></b>.
Gross profit was $51.8 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $37.5 million for the three-month period ended
March 31, 2025. Gross profit as a percentage of total revenue was 12.8% and 12.4% for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, and
the three-month period ended March 31, 2025, respectively. The change in trend is described under &#8220;Revenue&#8221; and &#8220;Cost
of services.&#8221;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><b><i>SG&amp;A
expense.</i></b> SG&amp;A expense, which represents costs associated with managing and supporting our operations, was $11.1 million
for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $11.8 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2025. SG&amp;A
expense as a percentage of total revenue was 2.7% and 3.9% for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, and March 31, 2025,
respectively. The period-over-period decrease in SG&amp;A expense was primarily attributable to lower spending on activities related
to the remediation of the Company&#8217;s material weakness, as the Company completed remediation as of June 30, 2025 and therefore incurred
significant remediation-related costs during the quarter ended March 31, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><b><i>Amortization
expense</i></b>. Amortization expense was $4.7 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $4.7 million for
the three-month period ended March 31, 2025. Amortization expense is driven mainly by acquired intangible assets resulting from
acquisitions.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><b><i>Interest
expense, net</i></b>. Interest expense, net, was $6.5 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $8.3 million
for the three-month period ended March 31, 2025. Interest expense, net, decreased period-over-period, due to lower debt levels during
2026 as compared to 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><b><i>Other income, net</i></b>.
Other income, net, was $1.4 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $1.1 million for the three-month period
ended March 31, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><b><i>Income tax expense.</i></b>
Income tax expense was $7.1 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $3.3 million for the three-month period
ended March 31, 2025. The decrease in effective tax rate period-on-period is primarily attributable to changes in geographic earnings
mix, as well as higher pre-tax income relative to adjustments related to the Company&#8217;s uncertain tax positions and unrecognized
tax benefits. See Note 7, <i>Income Taxes</i>, to our consolidated financial statements included in Item 1,
&#8220;Financial Statements,&#8221; of this Quarterly Report.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><b><i>Net income</i></b>.
Net income was $23.8 million for the three-month period ended March 31, 2026, compared to $10.4 million for the three-month period ended

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>
